      174 +const zaira: Gen = (b, ctx) => {
      175 +  b.ground(11.6, '#a99b80');
      176 +  // star fort: walls with arrow-head bastions
      177 +  const n = 6;
      178 +  const R = 9.2;
      179 +  const pts: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
      180 +  for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      181 +    const a = (k / n) * TAU;
      182 +    const a2 = ((k + 0.5) / n) * TAU;
      183 +    pts.push(V(Math.cos(a) * R, 0, Math.sin(a) * R));
      184 +    pts.push(V(Math.cos(a2) * (R + 2), 0, Math.sin(a2) * (R +
          +2)));
      185 +  }
      186 +  for (let k = 0; k < pts.length; k++) {
      187 +    const p = pts[k];
      188 +    const q = pts[(k + 1) % pts.length];
      189 +    const mid = p.clone().add(q).multiplyScalar(0.5);
      190 +    const len = p.distanceTo(q);
      191 +    const ang = Math.atan2(q.x - p.x, q.z - p.z);
      192 +    b.box(mid.x, 0, mid.z, 0.9, 3.4, len + 0.6, '#c4b291', { r
          +y: ang });

          +{ ry: ang });

      195 +  // inner city: houses on a grid with steps, railings and lam
          +pposts

       70 +    b.tower(Math.cos(a) * RW, Math.sin(a) * RW, 0.8, 5.6, '#cf

          + true });
       71 +  }

          +shut
       73 +  for (let k = 0; k < 7; k++) {

       75 +    const x = Math.cos(a) * RW;

       79 +    b.box(x, 0, z, 0.9, 1.4, 1.0, '#2a2420', { ry });

          +2, z: Math.sin(a) * (RW + 0.5) });
       81 +    bridge.group.rotation.y = ry;
       82 +    bridge.box(0, 0, 0.8, 0.85, 0.08, 1.6, '#7a5a3a');
       83 +    bridge.box(0, 0, 0.8, 0.95, 0.12, 0.1, '#5a4030');
       84 +    const ph = k * 0.9;
       85 +    bridge.on((t) => {
       86 +      const c = (t * 0.1 + ph / 7) % 1;
       87 +      const up = c < 0.5 ? smooth(clamp((c - 0.1) * 8, 0, 1))
          +: 1 - smooth(clamp((c - 0.6) * 8, 0, 1));

          +p - 0.5) * 2);
       90 +      bridge.group.rotation.x = -up * 1.35 + bounce;
       91 +    });
       92 +  }
       93 +  ctx.view.elev = 0.68;

          + 'glow', i: 0.9, seg: 6 });

      351 +// -----------------------------------------------------------
          +----- Zobeide
      352 +// A white city of streets wound like a skein, built to trap a
          + woman seen in a dream.
      353 +const zobeide: Gen = (b, ctx) => {
      354 +  b.ground(11.6, '#d8dce6');
      355 +  // maze on a square grid clipped to a circle
      356 +  const N = 11;
      357 +  const cs = 1.85;
      358 +  const half = (N * cs) / 2;
      359 +  const inside = (i: number, j: number) => Math.hypot((i + 0.5
          +) * cs - half, (j + 0.5) * cs - half) < 10.2;
      360 +  const visited = new Set<string>();
      361 +  const key = (i: number, j: number) => `${i},${j}`;
      362 +  // walls[i][j] = [east, south]
      363 +  const east: boolean[][] = Array.from({ length: N }, () => Ar
          +ray(N).fill(true));
      364 +  const south: boolean[][] = Array.from({ length: N }, () => A
          +rray(N).fill(true));
      365 +  const stack: [number, number][] = [[5, 5]];
      366 +  visited.add(key(5, 5));
      367 +  const path: [number, number][] = [[5, 5]];
      368 +  while (stack.length) {
      369 +    const [i, j] = stack[stack.length - 1];
      370 +    const nb = (
      371 +      [
      372 +        [i + 1, j],
      373 +        [i - 1, j],
      374 +        [i, j + 1],
      375 +        [i, j - 1],
      376 +      ] as [number, number][]
      377 +    ).filter(([a, c]) => a >= 0 && c >= 0 && a < N && c < N &&
          + inside(a, c) && !visited.has(key(a, c)));
      378 +    if (!nb.length) {
      379 +      stack.pop();
      380 +      if (stack.length) path.push(stack[stack.length - 1]);
      381 +      continue;
      382 +    }
      383 +    const [a, c] = nb[Math.floor(b.rng() * nb.length)];
      384 +    if (a > i) east[i][j] = false;
      385 +    if (a < i) east[a][c] = false;
      386 +    if (c > j) south[i][j] = false;
      387 +    if (c < j) south[a][c] = false;
      388 +    visited.add(key(a, c));
      389 +    stack.push([a, c]);
      390 +    path.push([a, c]);
      391 +  }
      392 +  const wallH = 1.3;
      393 +  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++)
      394 +    for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) {
      395 +      if (!inside(i, j)) continue;
      396 +      const x = (i + 0.5) * cs - half;
      397 +      const z = (j + 0.5) * cs - half;
      398 +      const white = b.jit('#f2f2f6', 0.01, 0.02, 0.03);
      399 +      if (east[i][j] && (i === N - 1 || inside(i + 1, j) || tr
          +ue)) b.box(x + cs / 2, 0, z, 0.32, wallH + (b.chance(0.25) ? 1
          +.0 : 0), cs + 0.32, white);
      400 +      if (south[i][j]) b.box(x, 0, z + cs / 2, cs + 0.32, wall
          +H + (b.chance(0.25) ? 1.0 : 0), 0.32, white);
      401 +      if (b.chance(0.12)) b.dome(x + cs / 2, wallH, z + cs / 2
          +, 0.45, '#f8f8fb', { seg: 10 });
      402 +      if (b.chance(0.08)) b.box(x + cs / 2 - 0.1, wallH, z + c
          +s / 2 - 0.1, 0.12, 0.6, 0.12, '#ffe2a8', { kind: 'glow', i: 1.
          +2 });
      403 +    }
      404 +  // outer white wall
      405 +  b.cyl(0, 0, 0, 10.8, 1.6, '#eef0f4', { seg: 64, open: true }
          +);
      406 +  b.cyl(0, 0, 0, 10.5, 1.6, '#e4e6ec', { seg: 64, open: true }
          +);
      407 +  // a minaret or two, and the moonlit domes
      408 +  b.tower(-0.4, -0.4, 0.4, 5, '#f4f4f8', { cap: 'spire' });
      409 +  b.dome(3.7, 0, 3.7, 1.3, '#fafafe', { seg: 18 });
      410 +  // men who came with the same dream wait at the corners
      411 +  for (let k = 0; k < 14; k++) {
      412 +    const [i, j] = path[Math.floor(b.r(path.length))];
      413 +    b.figure((i + 0.5) * cs - half + 0.4, 0, (j + 0.5) * cs -
          +half + 0.4, 0.9, '#2a2a3a');
      414 +  }
      415 +  // the woman of the dream, running, always escaping
      416 +  const runner = crowd(b, 6, () => '#ffffff', { kind: 'ghost',
          + opacity: 0.9 });
      417 +  const pts = path.map(([i, j]) => V((i + 0.5) * cs - half, 0.
          +05, (j + 0.5) * cs - half));
      418 +  b.on((t) => {
      419 +    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
      420 +      const u = (t * 0.9 - k * 0.18) % pts.length;
      421 +      const uu = u < 0 ? u + pts.length : u;
      422 +      const i0 = Math.floor(uu);
      423 +      const f = uu - i0;
      424 +      const p = pts[i0];
      425 +      const q = pts[(i0 + 1) % pts.length];
      426 +      runner.set(k, p.x + (q.x - p.x) * f, 0.05, p.z + (q.z -
          +p.z) * f, 1.25 - k * 0.08);
      427 +    }
      428 +    runner.commit();
      429 +    (runner.mesh.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial).opacity
          += 0.75;
      430 +  });
      431 +  // the trail she leaves fades
      432 +  ctx.view.elev = 0.85;
      433 +  ctx.view.dist = 30;
      434 +};

      127 +      b.beam(V(bx + b.r(-0.15, 0.15), 0, bz + b.r(-0.15, 0.15)
          +), V(bx, p.y, bz), 0.06, bamboo);
      128 +    }
      129 +    // cross bracing
      130 +    b.line(V(p.x - w / 2.3, 0.3, p.z - d / 2.3), V(p.x + w / 2
          +.3, p.y * 0.6, p.z - d / 2.3), '#6a5030');
      131 +    b.line(V(p.x + w / 2.3, 0.3, p.z + d / 2.3), V(p.x - w / 2
          +.3, p.y * 0.6, p.z + d / 2.3), '#6a5030');
      132 +    b.box(p.x, p.y, p.z, w, 0.1, d, '#9a7a4a');
      133 +    // shack of zinc or bamboo
      134 +    const sw = w * 0.65;
      135 +    const sh = 0.8 + b.r(0.6);
      136 +    const isZinc = i % 2 === 0;
      137 +    b.house(p.x - w * 0.12, p.z, sw, d * 0.7, sh, { y: p.y + 0
          +.1, wall: isZinc ? zinc : bamboo, roof: isZinc ? '#8a949c' : '
          +#7a6a3a', roofType: i % 3 === 0 ? 'cone' : 'gable', kind: isZi
          +nc ? 'metal' : 'solid', lit: 0.5 });
      138 +    // a balcony railing
      139 +    b.line(V(p.x - w / 2, p.y + 0.4, p.z + d / 2), V(p.x + w /
          + 2, p.y + 0.4, p.z + d / 2), '#5a4030');
      140 +    // water tank on legs
      141 +    if (i % 3 === 1) {
      142 +      const tx = p.x + w * 0.32;
      143 +      b.cyl(tx, p.y + 0.6, p.z, 0.28, 0.6, '#7a6a5a', { seg: 1
          +0 });
      144 +      for (const o of [-0.2, 0.2]) b.beam(V(tx + o, p.y + 0.1,
          + p.z), V(tx + o, p.y + 0.6, p.z), 0.03, '#5a4030');
      145 +    }
      146 +    // belvedere with conical roof
      147 +    if (i % 4 === 2) {
      148 +      const bx = p.x + w * 0.3;
      149 +      for (const [ox, oz] of [[-0.25, -0.25], [0.25, -0.25], [
          +-0.25, 0.25], [0.25, 0.25]]) b.beam(V(bx + ox, p.y + 0.1, p.z
          ++ oz), V(bx + ox, p.y + 1.3, p.z + oz), 0.025, bamboo);
      150 +      b.cone(bx, p.y + 1.3, p.z, 0.5, 0.6, '#b04a32', { seg: 8
          + });
      151 +    }
      152 +    // weather vane
      153 +    if (i % 2 === 1) {
      154 +      const v = b.sub({ x: p.x - w * 0.12, y: p.y + 0.1 + sh +
          + 0.7, z: p.z });
      155 +      v.cyl(0, 0, 0, 0.015, 0.6, '#3a3a3a', { seg: 4 });
      156 +      v.box(0.15, 0.5, 0, 0.35, 0.12, 0.01, '#c03a2a');
      157 +      vanes.push(v);
      158 +    }
      159 +    // jutting crane with pulley and a fishing pole
      160 +    if (i % 3 === 0) {
      161 +      const a = b.r(TAU);
      162 +      const end = V(p.x + Math.cos(a) * (w * 0.5 + 1.2), p.y +
          + 1.2, p.z + Math.sin(a) * (d * 0.5 + 1.2));
      163 +      b.beam(V(p.x, p.y + 0.1, p.z), end, 0.04, '#7a5a3a');
      164 +      b.line(end, V(end.x, end.y - 1.6, end.z), '#d8d0c0');
      165 +      b.cyl(end.x, end.y - 1.85, end.z, 0.12, 0.25, '#6a5a4a',
          + { seg: 6 });
      166 +    }
      167 +  });
      168 +  // ladders down to the ground and walkways between platforms
      169 +  plats.forEach((p, i) => {
      170 +    if (i % 2 === 0) ladder(b, V(p.x + 0.9, 0, p.z + 0.9), V(p
          +.x + 0.6, p.y, p.z + 0.6), 0.3, '#6a5030');
      171 +    let best = -1;
      172 +    let bd = 1e9;
      173 +    plats.forEach((q, j) => {
      174 +      if (j <= i) return;
      175 +      const d = Math.hypot(q.x - p.x, q.z - p.z);
      176 +      if (d < bd) {
      177 +        bd = d;
      178 +        best = j;
      179 +      }
      180 +    });
      181 +    if (best >= 0 && bd < 5.5) {
      182 +      const q = plats[best];
      183 +      walkway(b, V(p.x, p.y + 0.1, p.z), V(q.x, q.y + 0.1, q.z
          +), 0.35, 0.4, '#b89a62');
      184 +    }
      185 +  });
      186 +  b.on((t) => vanes.forEach((v, k) => (v.group.rotation.y = Ma
          +th.sin(t * 0.4 + k) * 1.2 + t * 0.05)));
      187 +  // inhabitants high up
      188 +  for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
      189 +    const p = plats[k % plats.length];
      190 +    b.figure(p.x + b.r(-0.5, 0.5), p.y + 0.1, p.z + b.r(-0.4,
          +0.4) + 0.3, 0.75, b.jit('#5a4a6a', 0.2, 0.3, 0.2));
      191 +  }
      192 +  ctx.view.elev = 0.34;
      193 +  ctx.view.ty = 4;
      194 +};
      195 +
      196 +// -----------------------------------------------------------
          +----- Armilla
      197 +// Nothing but pipes, rising where the walls should be, ending
          + in taps, showers and tubs; nymphs bathe there.
      198 +const armilla: Gen = (b, ctx) => {
      199 +  b.ground(11.6, '#9c9488');
      200 +  // traces of foundations
      201 +  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
      202 +    const a = (k / 8) * TAU + 0.2;
      203 +    const r = 5 + (k % 3) * 1.6;
      204 +    b.box(Math.cos(a) * r, 0, Math.sin(a) * r, 2.4, 0.15, 2.0,
          + '#b8b0a2', { ry: -a });
      205 +  }
      206 +  const copper = '#c88a5a';
      207 +  const lead = '#8a9098';
      208 +  const sprayFrom: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
      209 +  const porcelain = '#f6f4f0';
      210 +  const tub = (x: number, y: number, z: number, ry: number) =>
          + {
      211 +    b.box(x, y, z, 1.0, 0.42, 0.5, porcelain, { ry });
      212 +    b.box(x, y + 0.12, z, 0.85, 0.32, 0.36, '#6ab0d0', { ry, k
          +ind: 'water' });
      213 +    for (const [ox, oz] of [[-0.4, -0.18], [0.4, -0.18], [-0.4
          +, 0.18], [0.4, 0.18]]) {
      214 +      const c = Math.cos(ry);
      215 +      const s = Math.sin(ry);
      216 +      b.sphere(x + c * ox + s * oz, y - 0.04, z - s * ox + c *
          + oz, 0.06, '#d9a441', { kind: 'gold', seg: 5 });
      217 +    }
      218 +  };
      219 +  const basin = (x: number, y: number, z: number) => {
      220 +    b.lathe([[0, 0], [0.22, 0.02], [0.3, 0.18], [0.28, 0.2], [
          +0.18, 0.06], [0, 0.05]], x, y, z, porcelain, { seg: 14 });
      221 +  };
      222 +  const shower = (x: number, y: number, z: number) => {
      223 +    b.cone(x, y - 0.18, z, 0.16, 0.2, '#c8ccd0', { kind: 'meta
          +l', seg: 10 });
      224 +    sprayFrom.push(V(x, y - 0.2, z));
      225 +  };
      226 +  for (let k = 0; k < 16; k++) {
      227 +    const a = b.r(TAU);
      228 +    const r = Math.sqrt(b.r(0.05, 1)) * 9.5;
      229 +    const x = Math.cos(a) * r;
      230 +    const z = Math.sin(a) * r;
      231 +    const h = 2.5 + b.r(6);
      232 +    const mat = b.chance(0.5) ? copper : lead;
      233 +    b.cyl(x, 0, z, 0.08, h, mat, { kind: 'metal', seg: 8 });
      234 +    // branches at floor levels
      235 +    for (let f = 1.4; f < h; f += 1.5 + b.r(0.6)) {
      236 +      const ba = b.r(TAU);
      237 +      const len = 0.8 + b.r(1.2);
      238 +      const ex = x + Math.cos(ba) * len;
      239 +      const ez = z + Math.sin(ba) * len;
      240 +      b.beam(V(x, f, z), V(ex, f, ez), 0.06, mat, { kind: 'met
          +al', seg: 6 });
      241 +      b.sphere(x, f, z, 0.1, mat, { kind: 'metal', seg: 6 });
      242 +      const kind = b.ri(0, 3);
      243 +      if (kind === 0) {
      244 +        b.beam(V(ex, f, ez), V(ex, f + 0.6, ez), 0.05, mat, {
          +kind: 'metal' });
      245 +        shower(ex, f + 0.6, ez);
      246 +      } else if (kind === 1) {
      247 +        tub(ex, f - 0.42, ez, ba);
      248 +        b.box(ex, f - 0.62, ez, 0.08, 0.2, 0.08, mat, { kind:
          +'metal' });
      249 +      } else if (kind === 2) {
      250 +        basin(ex, f - 0.25, ez);
      251 +        b.box(ex, f - 0.12, ez, 0.04, 0.12, 0.04, '#d0d4d8', {
          + kind: 'metal' });
      252 +      } else {
      253 +        b.box(ex, f - 0.1, ez, 0.05, 0.12, 0.05, '#d0d4d8', {
          +kind: 'metal' });
      254 +        b.box(ex, f, ez + 0.06, 0.16, 0.03, 0.03, '#d9a441', {
          + kind: 'gold' });
      255 +        sprayFrom.push(V(ex, f - 0.12, ez));
      256 +      }
      257 +    }
      258 +    // a valve wheel and a gauge
      259 +    b.torus(x, 0.9, z + 0.1, 0.14, 0.02, '#c03a2a', { seg: 10,
          + tube: 4 });
      260 +  }
      261 +  // some tubs on the ground with nymphs
      262 +  const nymphs: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
      263 +  for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
      264 +    const a = (k / 6) * TAU + 0.4;
      265 +    const x = Math.cos(a) * 3.5;
      266 +    const z = Math.sin(a) * 3.5;
      267 +    tub(x, 0.05, z, -a);
      268 +    b.beam(V(x, 0, z + 0.4), V(x, 2.2, z + 0.4), 0.05, copper,
          + { kind: 'metal' });
      269 +    b.beam(V(x, 2.2, z + 0.4), V(x, 2.2, z), 0.05, copper, { k
          +ind: 'metal' });
      270 +    shower(x, 2.2, z);
      271 +    nymphs.push(V(x, 0.22, z));
      272 +  }
      273 +  const ny = crowd(b, nymphs.length, () => '#f8e8f0', { kind:
          +'glow' });
      274 +  b.on((t) => {
      275 +    nymphs.forEach((p, i) => ny.set(i, p.x + Math.sin(t * 0.6
          ++ i) * 0.15, p.y + Math.sin(t * 1.2 + i * 2) * 0.03, p.z, 0.75
          +, t * 0.2 + i));
      276 +    ny.commit();
      277 +  });
      278 +  // water spray glittering
      279 +  const N = sprayFrom.length * 10;
      280 +  const pts: THREE.Vector3[] = Array.from({ length: N }, () =>
          + V(0, 0, 0));
      281 +  const seeds = Array.from({ length: N }, () => [b.r(1), b.r(-
          +1, 1), b.r(-1, 1)]);
      282 +  const sp = animatedPoints(b, pts, () => '#bfe8ff', 0.16, ()
          +=> 1.2);
      283 +  sp.on((t) => {
      284 +    const pos = sp.points?.geometry.attributes.position as THR
          +EE.BufferAttribute;
      285 +    if (!pos) return;
      286 +    for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) {
      287 +      const s = sprayFrom[k % sprayFrom.length];
      288 +      const [ph, dx, dz] = seeds[k];
      289 +      const u = (t * 0.9 + ph) % 1;
      290 +      pos.setXYZ(k, s.x + dx * u * 0.25, s.y - u * u * 2.0, s.
          +z + dz * u * 0.25);
      291 +    }
      292 +    pos.needsUpdate = true;
      293 +  });
      294 +  ctx.view.elev = 0.35;
      295 +  ctx.view.ty = 3;
      296 +};
      297 +
      298 +// -----------------------------------------------------------
          +----- Sophronia
      299 +// Two half cities: the fair is permanent; the banks and schoo
          +ls are carted away each season.
      300 +const sophronia: Gen = (b, ctx) => {
      301 +  b.ground(11.6, '#9a9070');
      302 +  b.box(0, 0.01, 0, 0.2, 0.02, 22.6, '#e8d8b0');
      303 +  // ---- the permanent half: the fair (x < 0)
      304 +  // Ferris wheel
      305 +  const wx = -5.6;
      306 +  const wz = -3.2;
      307 +  const WR = 3.1;
      308 +  for (const o of [-0.5, 0.5]) {
      309 +    b.beam(V(wx - 1.6, 0, wz + o), V(wx, WR + 0.6, wz + o), 0.
          +07, '#d0d4d8', { kind: 'metal' });
      310 +    b.beam(V(wx + 1.6, 0, wz + o), V(wx, WR + 0.6, wz + o), 0.
          +07, '#d0d4d8', { kind: 'metal' });
      311 +  }
      312 +  const wheel = b.sub({ x: wx, y: WR + 0.6, z: wz });
      313 +  for (const o of [-0.35, 0.35]) wheel.torus(0, 0, o, WR, 0.05
          +, '#e8e0d0', { seg: 40, tube: 4, kind: 'metal' });
      314 +  const cars = 12;
      315 +  for (let k = 0; k < cars; k++) {
      316 +    const a = (k / cars) * TAU;
      317 +    wheel.beam(V(0, 0, -0.35), V(Math.cos(a) * WR, Math.sin(a)
          + * WR, -0.35), 0.025, '#e8e0d0', { kind: 'metal' });
      318 +    wheel.beam(V(0, 0, 0.35), V(Math.cos(a) * WR, Math.sin(a)
          +* WR, 0.35), 0.025, '#e8e0d0', { kind: 'metal' });
      319 +    wheel.sphere(Math.cos(a) * WR, Math.sin(a) * WR, 0.36, 0.0
          +8, '#ffd27a', { kind: 'glow', i: 2, seg: 5 });
      320 +  }
      321 +  const cages = crowd(b, cars, (k) => ['#e04a3a', '#3a8ae0', '
          +#f0c040', '#40b080'][k % 4]);
      322 +  wheel.on((t) => {
      323 +    const rot = t * 0.15;
      324 +    wheel.group.rotation.z = rot;
      325 +    for (let k = 0; k < cars; k++) {
      326 +      const a = (k / cars) * TAU + rot;
      327 +      cages.set(k, wx + Math.cos(a) * WR, WR + 0.6 + Math.sin(
          +a) * WR - 0.75, wz, 1.4);
      328 +    }
      329 +    cages.commit();
      330 +  });
      331 +  // roller coaster: a closed loop of humps
      332 +  const track: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
      333 +  for (let k = 0; k < 60; k++) {
      334 +    const u = (k / 60) * TAU;
      335 +    const x = -5 + Math.cos(u) * 4.2;
      336 +    const z = 3.6 + Math.sin(u) * 2.6 + Math.sin(u * 3) * 0.5;
      337 +    const y = 1.2 + Math.max(0, Math.sin(u * 3)) * 2.6 + Math.
          +sin(u) * 0.4;
      338 +    track.push(V(x, y, z));
      339 +  }
      340 +  b.tube(track, 0.07, '#e04a3a', { closed: true, seg: 180, kin
          +d: 'metal' });
      341 +  track.forEach((p, k) => {
      342 +    if (k % 3 === 0) b.beam(V(p.x, 0, p.z), p, 0.04, '#e8e0d0'
          +);
      343 +  });
      344 +  const coaster = crowd(b, 4, () => '#f0c040');
      345 +  const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(track, true);
      346 +  b.on((t) => {
      347 +    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      348 +      const u = (t * 0.07 - k * 0.012 + 10) % 1;
      349 +      const p = curve.getPoint(u);
      350 +      coaster.set(k, p.x, p.y + 0.06, p.z, 1.1);
      351 +    }
      352 +    coaster.commit();
      353 +  });
      354 +  // carousel
      355 +  const carousel = b.sub({ x: -1.9, z: -0.4 });
      356 +  carousel.cyl(0, 0, 0, 1.3, 0.2, '#e8d8b0', { seg: 16 });
      357 +  carousel.cyl(0, 0.2, 0, 0.12, 1.6, '#d9a441', { kind: 'gold'
          +, seg: 8 });
      358 +  carousel.cone(0, 1.8, 0, 1.45, 0.7, '#c03a4a', { seg: 16 });
      359 +  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
      360 +    const a = (k / 8) * TAU;
      361 +    carousel.beam(V(Math.cos(a) * 1.05, 0.2, Math.sin(a) * 1.0
          +5), V(Math.cos(a) * 1.05, 1.8, Math.sin(a) * 1.05), 0.02, '#d9
          +a441', { kind: 'gold' });
      362 +    carousel.sphere(Math.cos(a) * 1.05, 0.7, Math.sin(a) * 1.0
          +5, 0.14, '#f4efe6', { sy: 0.7, sz: 1.6, ry: -a, seg: 6 });
      363 +    carousel.sphere(Math.cos(a) * 1.45, 1.75, Math.sin(a) * 1.
          +45, 0.05, '#ffd27a', { kind: 'glow', i: 2, seg: 4 });
      364 +  }
      365 +  carousel.on((t) => (carousel.group.rotation.y = t * 0.6));
      366 +  // the globe of death with its crouching motorcyclist
      367 +  b.sphere(-8.6, 1.4, 0.8, 1.2, '#c8d0d8', { kind: 'glass', se
          +g: 14 });
      368 +  b.cyl(-8.6, 0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.3, '#5a5a5a', { seg: 12 });
      369 +  const rider = b.sub({ x: -8.6, y: 1.4, z: 0.8 });
      370 +  rider.sphere(1.05, 0, 0, 0.12, '#ff5a2a', { kind: 'glow', i:
          + 2, seg: 6 });
      371 +  rider.on((t) => {
      372 +    rider.group.rotation.y = t * 3.1;
      373 +    rider.group.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 1.3) * 1.2;
      374 +  });
      375 +  // ---- the temporary half: banks, school, factories, slaugh
          +terhouse (x > 0), lifted away
      376 +  const lifted: B[] = [];
      377 +  const places: [number, number, number, number, number, strin
          +g][] = [
      378 +    [3.2, -4.5, 2.2, 1.6, 2.2, 'bank'],
      379 +    [6.6, -3.0, 2.0, 1.8, 1.6, 'school'],
      380 +    [3.0, 0.4, 2.4, 1.8, 1.4, 'factory'],
      381 +    [7.0, 1.4, 2.0, 1.6, 2.6, 'palace'],
      382 +    [3.6, 4.6, 2.0, 1.6, 1.5, 'slaughterhouse'],
      383 +    [7.2, 5.4, 1.6, 1.4, 1.8, 'office'],
      384 +    [9.6, -1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.6, 'office'],
      385 +  ];
      386 +  places.forEach(([x, z, w, d, h, kind], i) => {
      387 +    b.box(x, 0, z, w + 0.4, 0.08, d + 0.4, '#7a7468');
      388 +    const s = b.sub({ x, z });
      389 +    s.box(0, 0, 0, w, h, d, kind === 'factory' ? '#8a7a6a' : '
          +#d8d0c0');
      390 +    if (kind === 'bank' || kind === 'palace') {
      391 +      for (let c = 0; c < 5; c++) s.cyl(-w / 2 + 0.2 + c * ((w
          + - 0.4) / 4), 0, d / 2 + 0.15, 0.08, h * 0.85, '#f0ebe0', { se
          +g: 8 });
      392 +      s.prism(0, h, d / 2 * 0, w + 0.2, 0.5, d + 0.3, '#e8e0d0
          +', { ry: Math.PI / 2 });
      393 +    } else if (kind === 'factory') {
      394 +      s.cyl(w / 3, h, 0, 0.15, 1.6, '#6a5a4a', { seg: 6 });
      395 +      for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) s.prism(-w / 3 + c * (w / 3)
          +, h, 0, w / 3, 0.4, d, '#6a5a4a');
      396 +    } else s.box(0, h, 0, w + 0.1, 0.1, d + 0.1, '#a89a88');
      397 +    s.windows(0, 0, 0, w, d, h, 0, '#ffe2a0', 0.5);
      398 +    // crane cables
      399 +    s.line(V(-w / 2, h + 0.2, 0), V(0, h + 3.4, 0), '#3a3a3a')
          +;
      400 +    s.line(V(w / 2, h + 0.2, 0), V(0, h + 3.4, 0), '#3a3a3a');
      401 +    s.line(V(0, h + 3.4, 0), V(0, 14, 0), '#3a3a3a');
      402 +    s.group.userData.ph = i * 0.14;
      403 +    lifted.push(s);
      404 +  });
      405 +  // the crane arms
      406 +  for (const [x, z] of [[5.2, -1.5], [5.5, 3.5]]) {
      407 +    b.box(x, 0, z, 0.3, 9, 0.3, '#e0a030');
      408 +    b.box(x, 9, z, 6, 0.25, 0.3, '#e0a030');
      409 +  }
      410 +  b.on((t) => {
      411 +    for (const s of lifted) {
      412 +      const c = (t * 0.035 + s.group.userData.ph) % 1;
      413 +      const up = c < 0.15 ? 0 : c < 0.45 ? smooth((c - 0.15) /
          + 0.3) : c < 0.7 ? 1 : 1 - smooth((c - 0.7) / 0.3);
      414 +      s.group.position.y = up * 6;
      415 +      s.group.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 0.5 + s.group.userData
          +.ph * 10) * 0.08 * up;
      416 +    }
      417 +  });
      418 +  ctx.view.yaw = 0.2;
      419 +  ctx.view.elev = 0.42;
      420 +  ctx.view.ty = 3;
      421 +};
      422 +
      423 +// -----------------------------------------------------------
          +----- Octavia
      424 +// The spider web city: a net over the abyss between two mount
          +ains, with everything hung beneath it.
      425 +const octavia: Gen = (b, ctx) => {
      426 +  // the abyss: dark, with mist far below
      427 +  b.ground(11.6, '#0c0a12', 0, 0.2);

          +cos(k * 3.1) * 0.5);
      223 +    }

      234 +  const web = (cx: number, cz: number, R: number, withHouses:
          +boolean, density: number) => {
      235 +    const corners: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
      236 +    const n = withHouses ? 9 : 10;

      246 +      } else {

      249 +        b.box(x, 0, z, 0.9, 0.05, 0.9, '#8a7a5a');
      250 +        corners.push(V(x, h + 0.4, z), V(x, h * 0.6, z));
      251 +      }
      252 +    }
      253 +    for (let k = 0; k < corners.length * density; k++) {
      254 +      const a = corners[Math.floor(b.r(corners.length))];
      255 +      const c = corners[Math.floor(b.r(corners.length))];
      256 +      if (a === c) continue;

      258 +      if (type < 3) b.line(a, c, colors[type], withHouses ? 1.
          +1 : 0.8);
      259 +      else {
      260 +        // black and white
      261 +        const m = a.clone().lerp(c, 0.5);
      262 +        b.line(a, m, colors[0], 1);
      263 +        b.line(m, c, colors[1], 1);
      264 +      }
      265 +    }
      266 +  };

      268 +  web(5.6, -5.2, 2.8, false, 4);

       91 +  for (let k = 0; k < 16; k++) moments.push(windows[(k * 7) %
          +windows.length]);

       93 +  const gold = new THREE.Color('#ffcf6a').multiplyScalar(1.6);
       94 +  const sparks = animatedPoints(
       95 +    b,
       96 +    moments.map((m) => m.clone()),
       97 +    () => '#ffe2a0',
       98 +    0.6,
       99 +    (i, t) => {
      100 +      const head = (t * 0.6) % moments.length;
      101 +      const d = (head - i + moments.length) % moments.length;
      102 +      return d < 1 ? 2.2 * (1 - d) + 0.1 : d < 4 ? 0.5 * (1 -
          +(d - 1) / 3) : 0.02;
      103 +    },
      104 +  );

      108 +      people.set(i, x, 0.02, p.z, 1, p.sp > 0 ? Math.PI / 2 :
          +-Math.PI / 2, 0.8);

          +g

      113 +    const head = (t * 0.6) % moments.length;
      114 +    const steps = 60;
      115 +    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) {
      116 +      const u0 = head - (s / steps) * 4;
      117 +      const u1 = head - ((s + 1) / steps) * 4;
      118 +      const P = (u: number) => {
      119 +        const uu = ((u % moments.length) + moments.length) % m
          +oments.length;
      120 +        const i0 = Math.floor(uu);
      121 +        const f = uu - i0;
      122 +        const a = moments[i0];
      123 +        const c = moments[(i0 + 1) % moments.length];
      124 +        return V(a.x + (c.x - a.x) * f, a.y + (c.y - a.y) * f
          ++ Math.sin(f * Math.PI) * 1.4, a.z + (c.z - a.z) * f);
      125 +      };
      126 +      const A = P(u0);
      127 +      const C = P(u1);
      128 +      const k = Math.pow(1 - s / steps, 1.5);
      129 +      thread.seg(A.x, A.y, A.z, C.x, C.y, C.z, gold.r * k, gol
          +d.g * k, gold.b * k);
      130 +    }
      131 +    thread.end();
      132 +  });
      133 +  void sparks;
      134 +  ctx.view.elev = 0.45;

  Ran 3 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● I built the visualization: a three.js atlas of all 55 cities, each one modeled
  from its own passage in the book and set on a lagoon at dusk. To run it:

  pnpm install
  pnpm dev

  What's in it
  - 55 different cities, grouped by the book's 11 themes. Some examples:
    - Octavia hangs in a net between two cliffs over a misty drop.
    - Valdrada stands over its upside-down twin in the lake.
    - Armilla is only pipes, bathtubs and spray.
    - Zemrude brightens or turns to gutters depending on how high you look from.
    - Penthesilea's center moves away from you.
    - Olinda grows ring by ring from a pinpoint.
    - Berenice nests just and unjust cities inside each other.
  - The Khan's garden is at the center: a pavilion, a chessboard, Kublai and
    Marco with their pipe smoke. The interludes between chapters are told here.
  - Guided journey: goes through all nine chapters in Marco's order with the
    interludes and an epilogue. Marco's boat sails the dotted route between the
    islands.
  - Three arrangements, and the cities glide between them:
    - Journey: a spiral in reading order.
    - Atlas: 11 themes × 5 cities.
    - Structure: the theme-by-number grid turned 45 degrees, so each row is one
      chapter. That's the actual hidden structure of the book.
  - Interface: an index with a theme filter, a card for each city, keyboard
    controls, a help panel, deep links like /#octavia, and a layout that works
    on phones. The sound is generated in the browser.

  Text: all city descriptions and interludes are my own short paraphrases, not
  quotes from the book. A first draft I wrote came too close to the published
  translation, so I rewrote all of it.

  How I checked it: type-check and production build pass. I took
  headless-browser screenshots of every city and of the intro, cards,
  interludes, index and mobile layout. A script visiting all 56 places (the 55
  cities plus the garden) found no console errors and steady 60 fps on this
  machine. That machine's GPU is decent, so weaker hardware may run slower. I
  couldn't hear the audio, so the sound is untested by ear.

  I stopped the dev servers I had running. The scripts/ folder has the
  screenshot and test tools I used; they expect Chromium at /usr/bin/chromium.
